Title: Propagation of the Gospel in the East: being an Account of the Success of two Danish Missionaries, lately sent to the East-Indies, for the Conversion of the Heathens in Malabar, in several Letters to their Correspondents in Europe; rendered into English from the High-Dutch and dedicated to the Society for the Propagation of the Gospel in Foreign Parts; three parts
Creator: Bartholomäus Ziegenbalg (1683-1719), Heinrich Plütschau (1676-1747) and Johann Ernst Gründler (1677-1720); translated by Anton Wilhelm Böhm
Publisher: London: Joseph Downing
Publication Date: 1718
Rights: Public Domain (published 1718, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item propagationofgos00zieg (https://archive.org/details/propagationofgos00zieg), the item's own propagationofgos00zieg_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: John Carter Brown Library; host metadata date: 1718. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-16. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 657b53c5a9ac06a0baa53fa3de8b23eca74b18d96f870ede4cea8ddd73b7b420.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Scan begins at the title page; long s read as f; right margins clipped on some pages.
Content note: The Halle-trained Tranquebar missionaries' own letters (1706-17) in Böhm's contemporary English, dedicated to the SPG: the English witness to the Halle-SPCK mission network.

They  have  many  Books,  which  they  pretend  to
